## Summary
<p>This is a gateway-only <code>extended-stable</code> release, which is our current equivalent to LTS. This release is OpenClaw from the end of October 2026, plus critical security updates, reliability and performance fixes, and features like new model support. The latest version of OpenClaw at the time of this release is <a href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/releases#release-v2026.11.10">2026.11.10</a></p>
<h2>2026.10.34</h2>
<p>This extended-stable patch updates the Gateway and official npm plugins from 2026.10.33. It includes the core npm package and Gateway Docker images, but no native-app releases.</p>
<h3>Highlights</h3>
<ul>
<li>OpenAI and ClawRouter: select GPT-6.2 Astra and Sol, with system-prompt caching on the OpenAI API-key and Sign in with ChatGPT Responses routes. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6452341389" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/200766" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/200766/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/200766">#200766</a>, <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6463385754" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/201094" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/201094/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/201094">#201094</a>) Authored by <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/steipete/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/steipete">@steipete</a>. <h3>Changes</h3>
<ul>
<li>MiniMax: stop offering retired M2 models, including during live discovery, and use MiniMax-M3 for PDF text extraction. If your configuration explicitly selects an M2 model, switch it to a supported model. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6555050786" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/203998" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/203998/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/203998">#203998</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/RomneyDa/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/RomneyDa">@RomneyDa</a>.</li>
</ul>

<ul>

## Why It Matters
Source signal: <p>This is a gateway-only <code>extended-stable</code> release, which is our current equivalent to LTS. This release is OpenClaw from the end of October 2026, plus critical security updates, reliability and performance fixes, and features like new model support. The latest version of OpenClaw at the time of this release is <a href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/releases#release-v2026.11.10">2026.11.10</a></p>
<h2>2026.10.34</h2>
<p>This extended-stable patch updates the Gateway and official npm plugins from 2026.10.33. It includes the core npm package and Gateway Docker images, but no native-app releases.</p>
<h3>Highlights</h3>
<ul>
<li>OpenAI and ClawRouter: select GPT-6.2 Astra and Sol, with system-prompt caching on the OpenAI API-key and Sign in with ChatGPT Responses routes. <h3>Changes</h3>
<ul>
<li>MiniMax: stop offering retired M2 models, including during live discovery, and use MiniMax-M3 for PDF text extraction. If your configuration explicitly selects an M2 model, switch it to a supported model. (<a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="6555050786" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/issues/203998" data-hovercard-type="pull_request" data-hovercard-url="/openclaw/openclaw/pull/203998/hovercard" href="https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/203998">#203998</a>) Thanks <a class="user-mention notranslate" data-hovercard-type="user" data-hovercard-url="/users/RomneyDa/hovercard" data-octo-click="hovercard-link-click" data-octo-dimensions="link_type:self" href="https://github.com/RomneyDa">@RomneyDa</a>.</li>
</ul>
<h3>Fixes</h3>
<ul>
<li>Access controls: apply the session permission mode consistently on Gateway tool calls, recheck bound Discord DMs against current policy, and keep Signal view-once attachments out of assistant context.
